# Discovery corpus — the boards the remaining work has to read

Captured in one pass so the passes that follow can plan for free. Every string
is the node's own `characters`, verbatim. A section marked PARTIAL hit the
response cap and is NOT complete — do not plan against it without re-reading.

  T	2838:12116	0,445	"URL changed — add a 301 from /home to /about-us so existing links keep working."
  T	2838:12117	0,471	"Add redirect"
  T	2838:12118	430,726	"UX-B-22 — the Title field is a DEFAULT, not a value. Derived from the page name it renders as a placeholder and is stored only if the user types one. Today usePageSettings.ts:106 pre-fills it from page.name and :275 writes it on the first save, after which renaming the page silently stops changing the search title."
  T	2838:12119	430,782	"UX-B-10 — a slug another page already uses shows its error beside the field and nothing else. The 500ms autosave is HELD while the form is invalid and no toast fires; the refusal toast is what re-renders the form and restarts the timer, roughly twice a second, forever (PageSettingsDrawer.tsx:41-45, usePageSettings.ts:248-261, Toast.tsx:151-153)."
  T	2838:12120	430,838	"UX-B-19 — one SCORE, one definition. The Listings table subtracts from 100 and penalises a title that falls back to the page name; this drawer adds up from 0 and pre-fills that same page name as the title, so a row reading 85 opens a panel reading something else (SearchListingsTable.tsx:39-61, seoScore.ts:28-39, usePageSettings.ts:106)."

  T	2838:12110	28,88	"✕"
  T	2838:12125	0,465	"A URL that does not load says so here. Today the preview img carries alt=\"\" and no error handling (SocialTab.tsx:28-30), so a wrong address shows an empty frame and the user cannot tell why."
  T	2838:12128	430,726	"UX-B-30 — both tabs behave the same way: the counter colours by range and the field warns at the limit. One counter meaning two different things in one modal is the defect."

  T	2838:12138	0,491	"Will publish — this page ships with the next deploy. Nothing is stored until you choose otherwise."
  T	2838:12139	430,726	"UX-B-02 — Draft is a real visibility here. scheduled and error are deleted (nothing writes them and no schedule exists); external is a kind of ROW, not a publication state, and keeps the glyph PageRow.tsx:242-245 already draws."
  T	2838:12140	430,782	"UX-B-03 — visibility is written ONLY when this control is used. Seeding it as password ? 'password' : hidden ? 'hidden' : 'live' and writing it on every autosave (usePageSettings.ts:103-104, :271) is what publishes a page during a typo fix in the SEO tab."
  T	2838:12141	430,838	"UX-B-23 — this board already draws Canonical URL and the product renders no input for it. The save writes a fresh seo object of seven fields and PageManager.ts:156 merges one level above, so canonicalUrl, structuredData and the four twitter* fields (project.ts:233-247) are deleted the first time anyone edits a meta description. Merge the object, and give the six orphan fields either an input or a deletion."

  T	2474:12049	232,210	"Page › Hero › Grid"

  T	2474:12051	238,228	"d3"
  T	2474:12058	616,240	"edge + centre"
  T	2474:12062	24,268	"What none of this says on its own"
  T	2474:12063	24,290	"The whole visual block is gated on there being a RESOLVED drop target. Over empty canvas there is no affordance at all — neither the valid line nor the invalid outline. A designer reading the five specimens above would reasonably assume the invalid state always answers a bad drop; it does not answer a drop that resolves to nothing."
  T	2474:12064	24,344	"And the refusal is late. The Layers tree draws its accent insertion line on rows that will REJECT the drop — legality is only checked at drop time, so the accept cue is shown first and the reason arrives afterwards as a 3-second band."

  T	2474:12066	24,20	"Canvas · element manipulated — resize · rotate"
  T	2474:12067	24,44	"Rotation is the largest fully-shipped canvas capability with no drawing anywhere in the file. Built as an anatomy board rather than the specified 1440×900 shell clone: a half-edited shell claims to be a screen while only its middle is true."
  T	2474:12068	40,84	"above 50px on both axes — eight handles"
  T	2474:12082	480,84	"under 50px on an axis — corners only"
  T	2474:12081	322,259	"240 × 140"
  T	2474:12090	480,264	"N/S edge handles need width > 50px; W/E need height > 50px. This element is 44 wide, so it gets neither."
  T	2474:12091	24,316	"The rotation handle"
  T	2474:12092	24,338	"A 12px white circle on a 1px stem, 24px above the selection box. role=\"slider\", aria-valuemin=0, aria-valuemax=360, title \"Drag to rotate (Shift for 15° snap)\". The maths is an atan2 delta from the element centre normalised to 0–360, and it writes transform: rotate(Ndeg). Hidden for a multi-selection and for a locked element — and a locked element cannot be selected at all, so in practice the lock case is unreachable from the canvas."

  T	2875:12440	470,78	"Six overlay chords bind globally and plain ⌘R toggles Rulers (CanvasFooterToolbar.tsx:271); ⌘B and ⌘' sit on the same footing.   T	2875:12435	16,180	"One zoom owner · one step · one clamp"
  T	2875:12441	470,180	"Two built surfaces with no door"
  T	2875:12436	16,198	"The footer flyout adds a fixed 10 (Canvas.tsx:408-414); the floating bar walks the presets 10/25/50/75/100/150/200/400 (CanvasFooterToolbar.tsx:258-264); the command registry is a third.   T	2875:12442	470,198	"useInspectorMode is read and persisted and the component that sets it renders nowhere — the mode is stuck at its stored value and the orphan also binds bare “I”, the AI panel’s key (UX-F-19).
